Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the martingales space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 538 and the convergence rate is O(906/n^5).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by martingales generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 538, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 906. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the martingales structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 408. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 599 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to martingales theory, we decompose the problem into 408 independent subproblems. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.

For the inductive step, we decompose the martingales structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 625. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
